## Trial and Error – Last Chance Grand Prix Trial Winners

*by Nate Price*

As the **Magic** season drags on, players are on a constant quest for whatever small scraps of information they can use to give them an edge. With Grand Prix-Washington DC just barely in the books, the players preparing for Grand Prix-Sendai haven't had much time to adjust things based on the information they gleaned from the results in the States. However, within the meager two weeks, the ripples from DC have grown and affected the format in some interesting ways.

Amidst the decks that have become staples of the most recent incarnation of the format ( [Vengevine](https://web.archive.org/web/20100604000000/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Vengevine) Naya, Mythic Conscription, Jund, UW Tapout, and UWR Planeswalkers), a few less well-known decks have surfaced here in Sendai. The first that caught my eye as I scanned the winning lists was an Allies deck piloted by Takuya Maekawa. It's a hyper-aggressive red/white/green deck relying on the cheap allies of those colors to beat opponents into submission. In addition to the efficient beaters, [Kabira Evangel](https://web.archive.org/web/20100604000000/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Kabira+Evangel) provides a path through blockers, while [Harabaz Druid](https://web.archive.org/web/20100604000000/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Harabaz+Druid) provides some mana acceleration and fixing. Outside the Ally shell, [Ranger of Eos](https://web.archive.org/web/20100604000000/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Ranger+of+Eos) can be used to refill his hand or provide him access to the copies of [Goblin Bushwhacker](https://web.archive.org/web/20100604000000/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Goblin+Bushwhacker) to enhance his team further. Lastly, since virtually every creature in his deck has an effect upon entering the battlefield, [Bloodbraid Elf](https://web.archive.org/web/20100604000000/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Bloodbraid+Elf) is always a hit, especially if it snags a [Naya Charm](https://web.archive.org/web/20100604000000/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Naya+Charm) to clear the way for attackers.

Another deck that caught my eye was an updated version of the graveyard decks that were mostly experimented with at the beginning of the season. Youichi Nagami relied on the power of [Hedron Crab](https://web.archive.org/web/20100604000000/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Hedron+Crab) , [Merfolk Looter](https://web.archive.org/web/20100604000000/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Merfolk+Looter) , and the new [Enclave Cryptologist](https://web.archive.org/web/20100604000000/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Enclave+Cryptologist) to sift through his deck, while relying on creatures that return from the graveyard to provide the muscle. Just in case things get dicey, an [Eldrazi Monument](https://web.archive.org/web/20100604000000/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Eldrazi+Monument) can lift the team to the air or provide an impenetrable wall of creatures for defense. Cards like [Bloodghast](https://web.archive.org/web/20100604000000/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Bloodghast) and [Vengevine](https://web.archive.org/web/20100604000000/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Vengevine) pull double duty here, acting as sources of damage, excellent discard to the various looters in the deck, and recursive sacrifices to the Monument. If things need to be cleared out of the way, [Sedraxis Alchemist](https://web.archive.org/web/20100604000000/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Sedraxis+Alchemist) can push it out of the way, which is a great way to deal with an [Eldrazi Conscription](https://web.archive.org/web/20100604000000/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Eldrazi+Conscription) or maybe even the one doing the conscripting- [Emrakul, the Aeons Torn](https://web.archive.org/web/20100604000000/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Emrakul%2C+the+Aeons+Torn) .

Here are all of the Grand Prix Trial Winning decklists for your perusal.
